# Daily video prompt pack — 15/11/2026 · Snake day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់)

Lucky signs: Snake, Ox, Rooster · Careful: Pig
Day element: Yin Water 💧 (Gui-si day; ធាតុទឹក) · every action scene today carries Water magic: flowing ribbons of clear blue water, rippling rings of light and cool silver-blue mist.
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky-numbers scene of about 10–12 seconds.
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Meaning scenes: in the love, work, money and horoscope scenes the zodiac guardian and its animal act out what Chanda is saying, the guardian protecting and caring and the animal trusting (no real-life people), and every scene with Chanda on camera says which feeling she shows.
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.
Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Today's horoscope – all 12 signs
Each sign's prediction for 15/11/2026: general luck, love, work, money and advice, in English and Khmer, ready to copy into your Facebook, TikTok, Telegram and YouTube posts. The videos speak the same predictions: the 60-second video (the Snake's luck and love), the 180-second video (love, work and money), the long videos (every sign) and the full-fortune video. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs, as in the videos.
Wording: the website's standard lines for each sign's rating (this date's written fortune is published on the website the day before; open the Studio at mybirthsign.com/studio/ then to use it).

## Luckiest animal today – Snake (ម្សាញ់) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color yellow (លឿង) · best time 01:00–03:00 · lucky numbers 9 · 28 · 929 · 8876 · 84229 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a photorealistic hero image of @Snake (the Snake guardian with their snake), the Snake of the Chinese zodiac, as a majestic celestial guardian from a high-budget fantasy epic. The snake symbolizes wisdom, intuition and quiet elegance: a wise, calm and elegant expression and posture that show this, mouth calm and closed. Realistic anatomy and textures, engraved gold ornaments with physically real metallic reflections. It stands on a round stone platform in the middle of a lotus pond in front of a temple inspired by Angkor Wat, with lotus lanterns floating around it, under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky; moonbeams fall on the snake, golden particles and flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light swirl around it. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around it. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cartoon or plastic look, no cropped body parts.

Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. ## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Snake, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Snake as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Snake's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Snake and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Snake? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់មែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?

## Voice-over – 180-second video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Snake? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Sunday, 15 November 2026, is a Snake day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Snake. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Wise and graceful, the Snake symbolizes wisdom, intuition, and quiet elegance. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Snake are wise, intuitive, and elegant. Snakes are deep thinkers who plan carefully before acting.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Snake? Your luck is strong today. Stay wise, calm and elegant. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Money today: A favourable day for steady income. Save a small part of today's earnings before treating yourself.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is yellow. Wear something yellow, or carry a small yellow item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 1 to 3 in the early morning. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the northeast. When you start something new, face the northeast, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 9, 28, and 929. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 9, 28, 929!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Snake's lucky days of the week are Wednesday and Saturday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Snake can be secretive or prone to overthinking. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: yellow. Best time: from 1 to 3 in the early morning. Lucky direction: northeast. Lucky numbers: 9, 28, and 929.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់ សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។ អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំបួន លេខម្ភៃប្រាំបី និងលេខប្រាំបួនរយម្ភៃប្រាំបួន។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

### Subtitles – Khmer (2026-11-15-180s.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,647

2
00:00:02,697 --> 00:00:07,478
នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ

3
00:00:07,528 --> 00:00:09,950
ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។

4
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,578
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ ទី 15 ខែវិច្ឆិកា

5
00:00:12,628 --> 00:00:14,841
ឆ្នាំ 2026 គឺជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។

6
00:00:14,891 --> 00:00:18,125
នេះជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។

7
00:00:18,175 --> 00:00:19,950
ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក!

8
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:21,905
ពស់ ឈ្លាសវៃ និងស្រស់ស្អាត។

9
00:00:21,955 --> 00:00:24,687
ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់តំណាងឲ្យប្រាជ្ញា វិចារណញាណ

10
00:00:24,737 --> 00:00:26,867
និងភាពថ្លៃថ្នូរដ៏ស្ងប់ស្ងាត់។

11
00:00:26,917 --> 00:00:29,950
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថាមពលល្អទាំងនេះ កំពុងនៅជាមួយអ្នក។

12
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:33,858
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ជាមនុស្សឈ្លាសវៃ

13
00:00:33,908 --> 00:00:36,042
ចេះវិភាគ និងសមរម្យ។

14
00:00:36,092 --> 00:00:39,950
ម្សាញ់គិតដិតដល់មុននឹងធ្វើសកម្មភាព។

15
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,341
សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ថ្ងៃនេះ

16
00:00:42,391 --> 00:00:44,298
សំណាងរបស់អ្នកល្អខ្លាំងណាស់។

17
00:00:44,348 --> 00:00:46,907
សូមរក្សាចិត្តឲ្យស្ងប់ ទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ

18
00:00:46,957 --> 00:00:49,950
ហើយចែករំលែកភាពរីករាយ ដល់មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន។

19
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,450
ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារថ្ងៃនេះ៖

20
00:00:52,500 --> 00:00:56,950
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា

21
00:00:57,000 --> 00:00:59,950
និងបង្ហាញក្ដីស្រឡាញ់ដោយបើកចំហ។

22
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,987
ការងារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ អតិថិជន

23
00:01:02,037 --> 00:01:05,506
និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។

24
00:01:05,556 --> 00:01:08,006
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់បង្ហាញគំនិត

25
00:01:08,056 --> 00:01:09,950
និងបិទកិច្ចព្រមព្រៀង។

26
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:14,293
លុយកាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ចំណូលនឹងនរ។

27
00:01:14,343 --> 00:01:18,031
សូមសន្សំប្រាក់ចំណូលថ្ងៃនេះមួយចំណែកតូច

28
00:01:18,081 --> 00:01:19,950
មុននឹងចាយលើខ្លួនឯង។

29
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,923
ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌លឿង។

30
00:01:22,973 --> 00:01:26,076
សូមស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌លឿង ឬយករបស់ពណ៌លឿងមួយ

31
00:01:26,126 --> 00:01:29,950
ជាប់ខ្លួន ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

32
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,314
ពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ

33
00:01:32,364 --> 00:01:34,768

34
00:01:34,818 --> 00:01:37,405
ជាពេលល្អ សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ

35
00:01:37,455 --> 00:01:39,950
ការជួបជុំ ឬការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

36
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,831
ទិសសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺទិសឦសាន។

37
00:01:42,881 --> 00:01:46,475
ពេលចាប់ផ្ដើមធ្វើអ្វីថ្មី សូមបែរមុខទៅទិសឦសាន

38
00:01:46,525 --> 00:01:49,950
ហើយបោះជំហានទៅមុខ ដោយចិត្តស្ងប់ និងរីករាយ។

39
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:53,677
សម្រាប់លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 9 លេខ 28 និងលេខ 929។

40
00:01:53,727 --> 00:01:57,314
សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់

41
00:01:57,364 --> 00:01:59,950
ហើយតោះនិយាយម្ដងទៀត៖ 9 28 929!

42
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:01,968
ថ្ងៃសំណាងប្រចាំសប្ដាហ៍

43
00:02:02,018 --> 00:02:05,455
របស់ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ គឺថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍។

44
00:02:05,505 --> 00:02:07,198
សូមចងចាំថ្ងៃទាំងនេះ

45
00:02:07,248 --> 00:02:09,950
សម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ របស់អ្នក។

46
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,986
សូមរំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់

47
00:02:13,036 --> 00:02:15,843
ជួនកាលសម្ងាត់ខ្លួន ឬគិតច្រើនពេក។

48
00:02:15,893 --> 00:02:17,986
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព

49
00:02:18,036 --> 00:02:19,950
និងស្ដាប់ចិត្តខ្លួនឯង។

50
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,514
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ

51
00:02:22,564 --> 00:02:26,446
ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

52
00:02:26,496 --> 00:02:29,950
ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

53
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:31,306
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់

54
00:02:31,356 --> 00:02:34,526
សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។

55
00:02:34,576 --> 00:02:37,662
អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង

56
00:02:37,712 --> 00:02:39,950
ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

57
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,657
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

58
00:02:41,707 --> 00:02:43,039
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

59
00:02:43,089 --> 00:02:45,722
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

60
00:02:45,772 --> 00:02:47,186
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

61
00:02:47,236 --> 00:02:49,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 9 លេខ 28 និងលេខ 929។

62
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

63
00:02:52,308 --> 00:02:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

64
00:02:54,423 --> 00:02:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

65
00:02:56,282 --> 00:02:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

66
00:02:58,590 --> 00:02:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-15-180s.en.srt)

```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,677
Born in the Year of the Snake?

00:00:02,727 --> 00:00:04,768
Here's your lucky color

3
00:00:04,818 --> 00:00:07,677

4
00:00:07,727 --> 00:00:09,950
plus one gentle reminder.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,298
Today, Sunday, 15 November 2026

6
00:00:12,348 --> 00:00:13,435
is a Snake day.

7
00:00:13,485 --> 00:00:16,086
It is a Great Day for everyone born

8
00:00:16,136 --> 00:00:17,980
in the Year of the Snake.

9
00:00:18,030 --> 00:00:19,950
This is your day to shine!

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:23,401
Wise and graceful, the Snake symbolizes

11
00:00:23,451 --> 00:00:26,764

12
00:00:26,814 --> 00:00:29,950
Today, this good energy is with you.

13
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,807
People born in the Year of the Snake

14
00:00:32,857 --> 00:00:35,426
are wise, intuitive, and elegant.

15
00:00:35,476 --> 00:00:37,648
Snakes are deep thinkers who

16
00:00:37,698 --> 00:00:39,950
plan carefully before acting.

17
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,825
Born in the Year of the Snake?

18
00:00:41,875 --> 00:00:43,450
Your luck is strong today.

19
00:00:43,500 --> 00:00:45,200
Stay wise, calm and elegant.

20
00:00:45,250 --> 00:00:47,325
Say yes to good chances, and share

21
00:00:47,375 --> 00:00:49,950
your happiness with the people around you.

22
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,394
Love and family today:

23
00:00:52,444 --> 00:00:56,506
A good day to reach out, share a meal

24
00:00:56,556 --> 00:00:59,950
and show your affection openly.

25
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,969
Work today: Customers

26
00:01:02,019 --> 00:01:05,238
and coworkers respond well to you.

27
00:01:05,288 --> 00:01:08,315
A good day to present your ideas

28
00:01:08,365 --> 00:01:09,950
and close a deal.

29
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,611
Money today: A favourable day

30
00:01:12,661 --> 00:01:14,262
for steady income.

31
00:01:14,312 --> 00:01:16,831
Save a small part of today's

32
00:01:16,881 --> 00:01:19,950

33
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,820
Your lucky color today is yellow.

34
00:01:22,870 --> 00:01:24,646
Wear something yellow

35
00:01:24,696 --> 00:01:27,863
or carry a small yellow item with you

36
00:01:27,913 --> 00:01:29,950
to keep good luck close.

37
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,603
Your best time today

38
00:01:31,653 --> 00:01:34,578
is from 1 to 3 in the early morning.

39
00:01:34,628 --> 00:01:37,140
It is a good time for important

40
00:01:37,190 --> 00:01:39,950

41
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:41,431
Your lucky direction

42
00:01:41,481 --> 00:01:43,135
today is the northeast.

43
00:01:43,185 --> 00:01:45,209
When you start something new

44
00:01:45,259 --> 00:01:47,876

45
00:01:47,926 --> 00:01:49,950

46
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:53,350
For lucky numbers: 9, 28, and 929.

47
00:01:53,400 --> 00:01:56,450
Comment your lucky number below

48
00:01:56,500 --> 00:01:59,950
and let's say it again: 9, 28, 929!

49
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:03,128
The Snake's lucky days of the week

50
00:02:03,178 --> 00:02:05,651

51
00:02:05,701 --> 00:02:08,174
Keep them in mind for plans

52
00:02:08,224 --> 00:02:09,950
that matter to you.

53
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,078
A gentle reminder: people born

54
00:02:12,128 --> 00:02:14,985

55
00:02:15,035 --> 00:02:16,759
or prone to overthinking.

56
00:02:16,809 --> 00:02:18,177
Today, stay balanced

57
00:02:18,227 --> 00:02:19,950

58
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,080
Make a small wish today

59
00:02:22,130 --> 00:02:25,135
and take one kind step toward it.

60
00:02:25,185 --> 00:02:27,172
A peaceful heart helps

61
00:02:27,222 --> 00:02:29,950

62
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:31,470
Before the day ends
```

The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

## Story video – YouTube (about 5 minutes)
"The Golden Snake and the Moon Lantern" (ពស់មាស និងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ): an original fairy tale (5:00) starring today's luckiest animal. A new story every day: today the golden snake and Sovann the baby elephant must bring the light back before the full-moon festival. Today's lucky color, direction, best time, lucky numbers and lucky days are part of the story. Make the picture of Sovann once (prompt below) and add it, Chanda and the Snake picture as ingredients in Flow. Chanda opens the story on camera and narrates the rest off screen; Sovann and the animals never speak (their words are told by Chanda). 30 clips of 10 seconds, in order 1 to 30.

### Character prompt – Sovann the baby elephant (make once, keep it)
Create a photorealistic image of Sovann, a tiny, lifelike magical baby elephant with bright, expressive eyes, soft grey-pink skin, big floppy ears and a little golden headpiece, as a small magical companion in a high-budget fantasy epic: realistic anatomy and textures with a soft warm magical glow, sitting on a glowing pink lotus by a still pond at night, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat and an enormous full moon behind, golden dust in the air. Cinematic lighting, beautiful depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, centered. No text, no letters, no watermark, no cartoon or plastic look.

Background: a glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under an enormous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pool, golden particles. Big, simple composition with only two or three subjects. Keep the bottom-right corner free (YouTube puts the video length there). Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. ### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands on the moonlit temple terrace beside the lotus pond, facing the camera, smiling warmly and speaking directly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures, holding a glowing golden storybook as if about to begin a bedtime story. Behind her, @Snake (the Snake guardian with their snake) glows softly on its lotus pedestal; the zodiac characters look around curiously but do not speak. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward her face. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda; the snake is the saved Flow character @Snake. Her expression moves from curious to thrilled: she raises one hand to her chest in happy surprise, then smiles broadly at the camera. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical.

She says in Khmer: "នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!" Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak.

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden snake bring the light back before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the moon lantern grew dim. Its light was almost gone. Without it, tonight's full-moon festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden snake. Today was a Snake day, and the snake felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A baby elephant named Sovann came trotting over. "Little Snake," he said, "only you can bring the light back. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Wise and graceful, the Snake symbolizes wisdom, intuition, and quiet elegance. Sovann smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To relight the moon lantern, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the snake, and it set off on its adventure, with Sovann right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Snake are wise, intuitive, and elegant. Snakes are deep thinkers who plan carefully before acting.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was yellow, the snake's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The snake tied a yellow ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Sovann, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the northeast, the snake's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But on the path stood an old, shaky bridge. The snake stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Snake can be secretive or prone to overthinking.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Sovann came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," he said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the snake crossed the bridge. With every step, the planks glowed gold. On the other side, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Sovann. For the Snake today, that is from 1 to 3 in the early morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the snake rang the golden bells 9 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The snake climbed 28 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a shining pearl of moonlight. It was the heart of the moon lantern!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the pearl, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Wednesday and Saturday shone brightest, the Snake's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the pearl carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Sovann right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The snake placed the pearl inside the moon lantern. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, whoosh! A golden light burst into the sky!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The lotus pond glowed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The snake felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Snake, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: yellow. Best time: from 1 to 3 in the early morning. Lucky direction: northeast. Lucky numbers: 9, 28, and 929.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Sovann fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
ប៉ុន្តែព្រឹកនេះ ចង្កៀងនៃព្រះចន្ទ ស្រអាប់ទៅវិញ។ ពន្លឺរបស់វា ស្ទើរតែរលត់។ បើគ្មានវា ពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់យប់នេះ មិនអាចចាប់ផ្ដើមបានទេ។

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
នៅកណ្ដាលស្រះ ផ្កាឈូកមាសមួយបានរីក ហើយមានពស់មាសតូចមួយ ចេញមក។ ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ហើយពស់មានអារម្មណ៍ពិសេសណាស់។

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
កូនដំរីតូចមួយ ឈ្មោះសុវណ្ណ ដើរតម្រង់មក។ សុវណ្ណនិយាយថា "ពស់តូចអើយ មានតែអ្នកទេ ដែលអាចនាំពន្លឺត្រឡប់មកវិញ។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
ពស់ ឈ្លាសវៃ និងស្រស់ស្អាត។ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់តំណាងឲ្យប្រាជ្ញា វិចារណញាណ និងភាពថ្លៃថ្នូរដ៏ស្ងប់ស្ងាត់។ សុវណ្ណញញឹម។ នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ដើម្បីបំភ្លឺចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទឡើងវិញ វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
ពស់និយាយថា "តោះទៅ!" ហើយវាក៏ចេញដំណើរផ្សងព្រេង ដោយមានសុវណ្ណ នៅក្បែរខ្លួនជានិច្ច។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ជាមនុស្សឈ្លាសវៃ ចេះវិភាគ និងសមរម្យ។ ម្សាញ់គិតដិតដល់មុននឹងធ្វើសកម្មភាព។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។ មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។ វាគឺពណ៌លឿង ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់ពស់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។ ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។ វាចង្អុលទៅទិសឦសាន ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់ពស់។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ប៉ុន្តែនៅលើផ្លូវនោះ មានស្ពានចាស់មួយ ដែលរង្គើ។ ពស់ឈប់។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ជួនកាលសម្ងាត់ខ្លួន ឬគិតច្រើនពេក។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
សុវណ្ណមកជិត ហើយញញឹម។ សុវណ្ណនិយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។ ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។ អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។"

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
ម្ដងមួយជំហានៗ ពស់ឆ្លងកាត់ស្ពាន។ រាល់ជំហាន ក្ដារស្ពានភ្លឺពណ៌មាស។ នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។ តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។ នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
សុវណ្ណនិយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។" សម្រាប់ពស់ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ពស់វាយកណ្ដឹងមាស ចំនួនប្រាំបួន ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។ ទីង! ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ ពស់ឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនម្ភៃប្រាំបី កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគុជពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទ ភ្លឺចែងចាំង។ វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញគុជ មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គុជយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានសុវណ្ណ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ពស់ដាក់គុជចូលក្នុងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក វឺត! ពន្លឺមាសមួយ បាញ់ឡើងទៅលើមេឃ!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ស្រះផ្កាឈូក ភ្លឺចែងចាំងម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ ពស់មានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំបួន លេខម្ភៃប្រាំបី និងលេខប្រាំបួនរយម្ភៃប្រាំបួន។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងសុវណ្ណ ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំងដប់ពីរ និងលេខសំណាង នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។ សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។ សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!

```
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,185
ពស់ដាក់គុជចូលក្នុងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ។

91
00:03:53,235 --> 00:03:55,930
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

92
00:03:55,980 --> 00:03:57,303
បន្ទាប់មក វឺត!

93
00:03:57,353 --> 00:03:59,950
ពន្លឺមាសមួយ បាញ់ឡើងទៅលើមេឃ!

94
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,491
ស្រះផ្កាឈូក ភ្លឺចែងចាំងម្ដងទៀត។

95
00:04:02,541 --> 00:04:03,639
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

96
00:04:03,689 --> 00:04:06,835
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

97
00:04:06,885 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

98
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,147
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

99
00:04:13,197 --> 00:04:15,032
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

100
00:04:15,082 --> 00:04:17,819
ពស់មានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

101
00:04:17,869 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

102
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:22,323
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ថ្ងៃនេះ

103
00:04:22,373 --> 00:04:24,018
ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

104
00:04:24,068 --> 00:04:26,645
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

105
00:04:26,695 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

106
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,657
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

107
00:04:31,707 --> 00:04:33,039
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

108
00:04:33,089 --> 00:04:35,722
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

109
00:04:35,772 --> 00:04:37,186
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

110
00:04:37,236 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 9 លេខ 28 និងលេខ 929។

111
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,016
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

112
00:04:44,066 --> 00:04:46,543
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងសុវណ្ណ

113
00:04:46,593 --> 00:04:49,950
ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

114
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

115
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

116
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

117
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

118
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-11-15-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,195
Can one little golden snake bring

2
00:00:02,245 --> 00:00:05,052
the light back before the full moon rises?

3
00:00:05,102 --> 00:00:07,909
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:07,959 --> 00:00:09,950
in Cambodia, our story begins.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,240
But this morning

6
00:00:11,290 --> 00:00:13,337
the moon lantern grew dim.

7
00:00:13,387 --> 00:00:15,434
Its light was almost gone.

8
00:00:15,484 --> 00:00:17,934
Without it, tonight's full-moon

9
00:00:17,984 --> 00:00:19,950
festival could not begin.

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:21,749
In the middle of the pond

11
00:00:21,799 --> 00:00:23,259
a golden lotus opened

12
00:00:23,309 --> 00:00:25,777
and out came a little golden snake.

13
00:00:25,827 --> 00:00:27,288
Today was a Snake day

14
00:00:27,338 --> 00:00:29,950
and the snake felt something special.

15
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,565
A baby elephant named

16
00:00:31,615 --> 00:00:33,565
Sovann came trotting over.

17
00:00:33,615 --> 00:00:35,335
"Little Snake," he said

18
00:00:35,385 --> 00:00:38,027
"only you can bring the light back.

19
00:00:38,077 --> 00:00:39,950
Today is your lucky day!"

20
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,860
Wise and graceful, the Snake symbolizes

21
00:00:42,910 --> 00:00:45,696
wisdom, intuition, and quiet elegance.

22
00:00:45,746 --> 00:00:46,741
Sovann smiled.

23
00:00:46,791 --> 00:00:48,234
That was exactly why

24
00:00:48,284 --> 00:00:49,950
the moon had chosen it.

25
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,110
To relight the moon lantern

26
00:00:52,160 --> 00:00:54,350
the little hero needed three

27
00:00:54,400 --> 00:00:56,910
lucky treasures: its lucky color

28
00:00:56,960 --> 00:00:59,950
its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

29
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,939
"Let's go!" said the snake

30
00:01:02,989 --> 00:01:06,502
and it set off on its adventure

31
00:01:06,552 --> 00:01:09,950
with Sovann right by its side.

32
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,807
People born in the Year of the Snake

33
00:01:12,857 --> 00:01:15,426
are wise, intuitive, and elegant.

34
00:01:15,476 --> 00:01:17,648
Snakes are deep thinkers who

35
00:01:17,698 --> 00:01:19,950
plan carefully before acting.

36
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,189
The first treasure was waiting

37
00:01:22,239 --> 00:01:24,801
in a garden of a thousand lanterns.

38
00:01:24,851 --> 00:01:26,666
Only one shone brightest.

39
00:01:26,716 --> 00:01:28,607
It was yellow, the snake's

40
00:01:28,657 --> 00:01:29,950
lucky color today.

41
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,916
The snake tied a yellow

42
00:01:31,966 --> 00:01:33,882
ribbon around its neck.

43
00:01:33,932 --> 00:01:36,959
"Wear your lucky color," said Sovann

44
00:01:37,009 --> 00:01:39,950
"and good luck stays close to you."

45
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,430
Next, they came to a crossroads

46
00:01:42,480 --> 00:01:43,710

47
00:01:43,760 --> 00:01:45,550
One path began to glow.

48
00:01:45,600 --> 00:01:47,710
It pointed to the northeast

49
00:01:47,760 --> 00:01:49,950
the snake's lucky direction.

50
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,369
But on the path stood

51
00:01:51,419 --> 00:01:52,788
an old, shaky bridge.

52
00:01:52,838 --> 00:01:54,004
The snake stopped.

53
00:01:54,054 --> 00:01:55,491
Sometimes, people born

54
00:01:55,541 --> 00:01:58,261
in the Year of the Snake can be secretive

55
00:01:58,311 --> 00:01:59,950
or prone to overthinking.

56
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,686
Sovann came close and smiled.

57
00:02:02,736 --> 00:02:05,139
"Breathe slowly," he said.

58
00:02:05,189 --> 00:02:06,837
"Trust your heart.

59
00:02:06,887 --> 00:02:09,950
You are stronger than you think."

60
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:11,851
Step by step, the snake

61
00:02:11,901 --> 00:02:13,421
crossed the bridge.

62
00:02:13,471 --> 00:02:16,727
With every step, the planks glowed gold.

63
00:02:16,777 --> 00:02:19,950
On the other side, it laughed with joy.

64
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,311
The little hero learned that its worries

65
00:02:23,361 --> 00:02:26,589
were small, and its good heart was big.

66
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950
Balance and courage always help us grow.

67
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

68
00:02:32,397 --> 00:02:34,413
stone door in the temple.

69
00:02:34,463 --> 00:02:36,562
Carved above it were bells

70
00:02:36,612 --> 00:02:39,950
stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

71
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,443
"The door opens only

72
00:02:41,493 --> 00:02:43,831
at your best time," said Sovann.

73
00:02:43,881 --> 00:02:45,249
For the Snake today

74
00:02:45,299 --> 00:02:48,308
that is from 1 to 3 in the early morning.

75
00:02:48,358 --> 00:02:49,950
They waited patiently.

76
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:51,502
When the time came

77
00:02:51,552 --> 00:02:54,864
the snake rang the golden bells 9 times

78
00:02:54,914 --> 00:02:56,847

79
00:02:56,897 --> 00:02:57,881
Ding!

80
00:02:57,931 --> 00:02:59,950
The door began to shine.

81
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:01,086
The door opened

82
00:03:01,136 --> 00:03:03,586
and a stairway of light appeared.

83
00:03:03,636 --> 00:03:06,162
The snake climbed 28 glowing steps

84
00:03:06,212 --> 00:03:07,905
its second lucky number

85
00:03:07,955 --> 00:03:09,950

86
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:12,250
At the top of the tower

87
00:03:12,300 --> 00:03:16,250
they found a shining pearl of moonlight.

88
00:03:16,300 --> 00:03:19,950
It was the heart of the moon lantern!

89
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,651
Around the pearl, seven little lights

90
00:03:22,701 --> 00:03:25,497
twinkled, one for each day of the week.

91
00:03:25,547 --> 00:03:28,271
Wednesday and Saturday shone brightest

92
00:03:28,321 --> 00:03:29,950
the Snake's lucky days.

93
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,969
Time was running out.

94
00:03:32,019 --> 00:03:34,565
Holding the pearl carefully

95
00:03:34,615 --> 00:03:37,258
the little hero hurried home

96
00:03:37,308 --> 00:03:39,950
with Sovann right beside it.

97
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting.

98
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538
The night was dark

99
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381
and the full moon was rising.

100
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950
Was it too late?

101
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,981
The snake placed the pearl

102
00:03:52,031 --> 00:03:53,856
inside the moon lantern.

103
00:03:53,906 --> 00:03:56,278
For a moment, nothing happened.

104
00:03:56,328 --> 00:03:57,294
Then, whoosh!

105
00:03:57,344 --> 00:03:59,950
A golden light burst into the sky!

106
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:02,104
The lotus pond glowed again.

107
00:04:02,154 --> 00:04:04,104
Lanterns rose into the sky

108
00:04:04,154 --> 00:04:06,642
and the full-moon festival began.

109
00:04:06,692 --> 00:04:07,873
Everyone cheered

110
00:04:07,923 --> 00:04:09,950
for the little golden hero!

111
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,669
The full moon shone down warmly

112
00:04:12,719 --> 00:04:14,687

113
00:04:14,737 --> 00:04:17,669
The snake felt peaceful and proud.

114
00:04:17,719 --> 00:04:19,950
It had done its very best.

115
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,311
If you were born in the Year of the Snake

116
00:04:23,361 --> 00:04:25,114

117
00:04:25,164 --> 00:04:27,737
Like our hero, be brave, be kind

118
00:04:27,787 --> 00:04:29,950

119
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,817
Let's remember today's luck.

120
00:04:31,867 --> 00:04:33,150
Lucky color: yellow.

121
00:04:33,200 --> 00:04:33,950
Best time:

122
00:04:34,000 --> 00:04:36,150
from 1 to 3 in the early morning.

123
00:04:36,200 --> 00:04:37,950
Lucky direction: northeast.

124
00:04:38,000 --> 00:04:39,950
Lucky numbers: 9, 28, and 929.

125
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:43,158
And so, under the bright full moon

126
00:04:43,208 --> 00:04:46,742

127
00:04:46,792 --> 00:04:49,950

128
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:51,617
That's your Chinese zodiac

129
00:04:51,667 --> 00:04:52,771
fortune for today.

130
00:04:52,821 --> 00:04:54,053
See all twelve signs

131

132
00:04:56,795 --> 00:04:58,860
Follow us for tomorrow's fortune.

133
00:04:58,910 --> 00:04:59,950
Have a lucky day!
```

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Pig (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Pig, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Pig clashes with the Snake day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Pig as ingredients (the Pig is the only zodiac character in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the pig hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little golden firefly keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Stop the scroll
   English: Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Pig? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.
   Khmer: ឈប់! កុំអូសរំលង។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ រាសីអ្នកថ្ងៃនេះមិនល្អទេ។ តែកុំបារម្ភ សូមមើលឲ្យចប់។
2. Warning
   English: Year of the Pig, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ចាំបន្តិច! ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃប៉ះឆុងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលវិធីឆ្លងកាត់ថ្ងៃនេះដោយស្ងប់។
3. Question
   English: Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Pig, here's why.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្វីៗហាក់ដូចជាមិនសូវស្រួលមែនទេ? បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ នេះជាមូលហេតុ។
★ 4. Send it to a friend
   English: Know someone born in the Year of the Pig? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.
   Khmer: ស្គាល់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរទេ? ផ្ញើវីដេអូនេះទៅគាត់ មុនពេលគាត់សម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។
5. Only one sign
   English: Out of all twelve signs, only one should slow down today: the Pig.
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗថ្ងៃនេះ គឺឆ្នាំកុរ។

## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Know someone born in the Year of the Pig? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 15 November, is a Snake day, opposite the Pig in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be overly trusting or indulgent. Pause before you react. Be kind to yourself.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Small steps still count.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Snake) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Ox and Rooster), the sign to take it easy (Pig) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients in Flow (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each part names its own character). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Snake
2:40 Good Day: Ox
3:30 Good Day: Rooster
4:20 Take it easy: Pig
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Pig? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរមែនទេ? សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ មុនធ្វើរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកផ្សេងទៀត សំណាងរបស់អ្នកក៏នៅទីនេះដែរ។
4. Name the winner
   English: Today the Snake has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់មានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ហើយមានមួយឆ្នាំគួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ តើជាឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
5. Comment first
   English: Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.
   Khmer: សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នកឥឡូវនេះ។ មុនវីដេអូនេះចប់ អ្នកនឹងដឹងពណ៌ ពេល និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video today (the hook changes from day to day, and each one gives every viewer a reason to stay to find their own sign). To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's picture and the saved zodiac characters (named after their animals) added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

On the right, smaller, @Pig (the Pig guardian with their pig), sleepy and calm inside a soft blue bubble of light, eyes half closed. ## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today is a Snake day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Snake, Ox and Rooster are lucky today, and the Pig should slow down. Find your sign in the chapters below.

Before we start, comment your sign below, and subscribe so you get your fortune every day. Share your luck.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
15 November belongs to the Snake. Year of the Snake, this Great Day is yours! A Yin Water day!

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Wise and graceful, the Snake symbolizes wisdom, intuition, and quiet elegance. Let it guide you.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)

ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។ សូមមើលបន្ត មានដំណឹងល្អៗទៀត។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា និងបង្ហាញក្ដីស្រឡាញ់ដោយបើកចំហ។ ថ្ងៃធាតុទឹក!

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
ការងារថ្ងៃនេះ៖ អតិថិជន និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់បង្ហាញគំនិត និងបិទកិច្ចព្រមព្រៀង។

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
លុយកាក់ថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ចំណូលនឹងនរ។ សូមសន្សំប្រាក់ចំណូលថ្ងៃនេះមួយចំណែកតូច មុននឹងចាយលើខ្លួនឯង។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ពណ៌សំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌លឿង។ សូមស្លៀក ឬយករបស់ពណ៌លឿង ជាប់ខ្លួន។ សូមឲ្យវារំលឹកអ្នក ឲ្យញញឹម និងមានទំនុកចិត្ត។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។ ពេលល្អបំផុត៖ ម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ សូមប្រើពេលនោះ សម្រាប់ការទូរស័ព្ទ និងការសម្រេចចិត្តធំៗ។

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
លេខសំណាង៖ ប្រាំបួន ម្ភៃប្រាំបី និងប្រាំបួនរយម្ភៃប្រាំបួន។ សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយរក្សាទុកវីដេអូនេះ!

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
ថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ គឺថ្ងៃពុធ និងថ្ងៃសៅរ៍។ សូមទុកសម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
រំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ជួនកាលសម្ងាត់ខ្លួន ឬគិតច្រើនពេក។ សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព។ សូមមើលបន្ត មានដំណឹងល្អៗទៀត។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ! ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។ ថ្ងៃធាតុទឹក!

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌លឿង ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ដល់ម៉ោងដប់មួយ ព្រឹក។ ជម្រើសតូចៗ នាំសំណាងធំ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។ លេខសំណាង៖ បួន ដប់មួយ និងបួនរយដប់បួន។ ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។ សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។ ថ្ងៃធាតុទឹក!

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំរកា៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ! ឆ្នាំរកា ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។ ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលបន្តិចជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌មាស ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី ទៀបភ្លឺ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសឦសាន។ លេខសំណាង៖ ប្រាំ ប៉ែតសិបប្រាំ និងប្រាំពីររយប៉ែតសិបប្រាំបី។ ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរកា ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។ សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទីដប់ប្រាំ ខែវិច្ឆិកា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ទល់មុខឆ្នាំកុរ ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។ មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។ មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។ ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលជឿគេពេក ឬបណ្ដោយចិត្តខ្លួនឯងពេក។ សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។ សូមមេត្តាខ្លួនឯង។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់ និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។ ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

Scene 31 (5:00–5:10)
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់ សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។ ជំហានតូចៗ ក៏មានតម្លៃដែរ។

Scene 32 (5:10–5:20)
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។ រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

Scene 33 (5:20–5:30)
ឆ្នាំជូត៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំបួន ព្រឹក។ ថ្ងៃធាតុទឹក!

Scene 34 (5:30–5:40)
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។ ថ្ងៃធាតុទឹក!

Scene 35 (5:40–5:50)
ឆ្នាំខាល៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ទឹកក្រូច។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងដប់មួយ ព្រឹក ដល់ម៉ោងមួយ រសៀល។

Scene 36 (5:50–6:00)
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងត្បូង។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់ ហើយរឿងល្អៗនឹងមកដល់។

Scene 37 (6:00–6:10)
ឆ្នាំថោះ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ផ្កាឈូក។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងមួយ ដល់ម៉ោងបី រសៀល។ ថ្ងៃធាតុទឹក!

Scene 38 (6:10–6:20)
ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសនិរតី។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 39 (6:20–6:30)
ឆ្នាំរោង៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ប្រាក់។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងដប់មួយ យប់ ដល់ម៉ោងមួយ ទៀបភ្លឺ។

Scene 40 (6:30–6:40)
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងជើង។ ជម្រើសល្អៗតូចៗ នាំមកនូវថ្ងៃដ៏សំណាង។

Scene 41 (6:40–6:50)
ឆ្នាំមមី៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងបី ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំ ទៀបភ្លឺ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 42 (6:50–7:00)
ស្នេហា៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់សម្រាប់ទំនាក់ទំនង។ លុយកាក់៖ ការចំណាយប្រចាំថ្ងៃ គួររក្សាឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។ សូមរក្សាចិត្តស្ងប់។

Scene 43 (7:00–7:10)
ឆ្នាំមមែ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ក្រហម។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 44 (7:10–7:20)
ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 45 (7:20–7:30)

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Photorealistic live-action fantasy film, the look of a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with the grandeur of a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Real human faces with natural skin texture, the zodiac characters saved in Flow, each named after its animal (@Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog, @Pig): a guardian warrior in ornate armor themed on the animal, with a lifelike animal beside them (realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales), always exactly as in the saved character picture, noble and powerful (never cute toys or cartoons). Setting: a majestic Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with towering carved stone, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with floating pink lotus flowers and ornate golden lanterns. A living, magical sky in every shot: thin silver clouds drift slowly across the moon, stars twinkle, a soft halo pulses around the moon and sky lanterns rise gently in the distance. Action and magic in the style of a Chinese fantasy (xianxia) drama: graceful wire-fu flying leaps, slow-motion martial-arts moves, flowing robes and capes, glowing sword energy and swirling qi light, always without blood, injury or fighting between characters. Golden magic: shimmering gold particles, flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light and floating gold dust that never hide a face. Physically real lighting: cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow, volumetric light rays; colors deep navy, imperial gold, ruby red and lotus pink. Cinematic depth of field, smooth slow camera movement, high-end visual effects, 4K detail. The zodiac guardians and their animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos, no cartoon or plastic look, no low-quality CGI.

## Characters (already saved in Google Flow)
Your characters are saved in Flow as @Chanda and the 12 zodiac characters named after their animals: @Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog and @Pig. For each scene, add Chanda and the characters the prompt names as ingredients; the prompts below point to them with @ (for example @Snake), so keep the names in Flow exactly the same.

### Character reference image prompts
- **Chanda (ចន្ទា), the presenter (make once, use in every video):** Create a photorealistic portrait from a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists. Realistic human proportions, natural facial expression with a gentle closed-lip smile, real silk that folds and catches the light, real gold jewelry with true metallic reflections. Graceful, upright posture, one hand held open toward the temple as if presenting it, the other resting at her waist. She stands barefoot on a carved sandstone temple terrace with an ornate stone balustrade, beside a reflecting pool with pink lotus flowers; behind her, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat glows under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, with golden lanterns and soft drifting gold dust. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light on her face and hair, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, sharp focus on her eyes, ultra-detailed. Composition: vertical 9:16, from the knees up, centered, facing the camera. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cartoon look, no plastic or airbrushed skin. Keep her face, hair, outfit, jewelry and proportions exactly the same in every image and video (see the approved picture characters/chanda.jpg).
- **Snake:** Create a photorealistic character portrait of an elegant, lifelike snake with shimmering gold scales and jewel-like eyes, the Snake of the Chinese zodiac, as a majestic celestial guardian from a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales with individual detail, expressive intelligent eyes, a calm closed mouth, and ceremonial gold ornaments with physically real metallic reflections. Noble and powerful, never a cute toy or cartoon mascot, with its own clear silhouette and personality. Setting: a carved sandstone temple terrace inspired by Angkor Wat at night, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, soft drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with pink lotus flowers and glowing golden lanterns; soft gold dust in the air. Light: cool silver-blue moonlight from behind, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow from below. Ultra-detailed, sharp focus on the eyes, cinematic depth of field, professional color grading. Composition: square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around it. Keep this exact design for every image and video. @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. Her expression moves from curious to thrilled: she raises one hand to her chest in happy surprise, then smiles broadly at the camera. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Snake (the Snake guardian with their snake) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it lifts its head high as its guardian moves through a slow, flowing sword form, her blade leaving shimmering silver trails that hang in the air like calligraphy, as today's Water magic swirls around them: flowing ribbons of clear blue water, rippling rings of light and cool silver-blue mist. Daily Chinese Zodiac Fortune – 15/11/2026 | Snake Day

### Description – Khmer
ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។ ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ និងឆ្នាំរកា មានសំណាងល្អជាងគេនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ ចំណែកឆ្នាំកុរ គួរសម្រាកឲ្យស្រួលខ្លួន ធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ និងកុំប្រញាប់ក្នុងការសម្រេចចិត្ត។

ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់៖ ពណ៌លឿង · 01:00–03:00
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ៖ ពណ៌លឿង · 09:00–11:00
ឆ្នាំរកា៖ ពណ៌មាស · 01:00–03:00
ឆ្នាំកុរ៖ ពណ៌ត្នោត · 05:00–07:00

លេខសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ សម្រាប់ឆ្នាំសំណាង (1 ដល់ 5 ខ្ទង់)៖
ឆ្នាំម្សាញ់  9 · 28 · 929 · 8876 · 84229
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ  4 · 11 · 414 · 1147 · 14111
ឆ្នាំរកា  5 · 85 · 788 · 7158 · 85754

លេខសំណាង (1 ដល់ 5 ខ្ទង់) សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺសម្រាប់ការកម្សាន្តប៉ុណ្ណោះ មិនមែនជាការទស្សន៍ទាយ ឬការណែនាំឱ្យភ្នាល់ទេ។

មើលរាសីទាំង 12៖ https://mybirthsign.com/km/

ការអានរាសីចិនតាមប្រពៃណី សម្រាប់ការកម្សាន្ត និងការឆ្លុះបញ្ចាំងខ្លួនប៉ុណ្ណោះ មិនមែនជាការព្យាករណ៍វិទ្យាសាស្ត្រទេ។

### Description – English
Today is a Snake day. Snake, Ox and Rooster are the luckiest signs today. Pig should take it easy, slow down and avoid rushing big decisions.

Snake: yellow · 01:00–03:00
Ox: yellow · 09:00–11:00
Rooster: gold · 01:00–03:00
Pig: brown · 05:00–07:00

Today's lucky numbers for the lucky signs (1 to 5 digits):
Snake  9 · 28 · 929 · 8876 · 84229
Ox  4 · 11 · 414 · 1147 · 14111
Rooster  5 · 85 · 788 · 7158 · 85754

Lucky numbers are for fun only, not a prediction or betting advice.

All 12 signs: https://mybirthsign.com

Traditional Chinese zodiac reading for entertainment and self-reflection only, not a scientific prediction.

#ChineseZodiac #DailyHoroscope #YearOfTheSnake #LuckyNumbers #Shorts
